* is used as a Wildcard.  A few Examples:\n\nfootsteps      matches the sound name exactly\na loud ba*     matches sounds beginning with 'a loud ba'\n*losion!       matches sounds ending with 'losion!'\na *oud*ba*     multiple * are allowed\n*LoU*bA*       case insensitive search
* is used as a Wildcard.  A few Examples:\n\nhuman          matches every NPC\nzombie         matches the monster name exactly\nacidic zo*     matches monsters beginning with 'acidic zo'\n*mbie          matches monsters ending with 'mbie'\n*cid*zo*ie     multiple * are allowed\nAcI*zO*iE      case insensitive search
* is used as a Wildcard.  A few Examples:\n\nwooden arrow    matches the itemname exactly\nwooden ar*      matches items beginning with wood ar\n*rrow           matches items ending with rrow\n*avy fle*fi*arrow     multiple * are allowed\nheAVY*woOD*arrOW      case insensitive search\n\nPickup based on item materials:\nm:kevlar        matches items made of Kevlar\nM:copper        matches items made purely of copper\nM:steel,iron    multiple materials allowed (OR search)
